- **§22 M3 (the gate as met)**: "the label-set and verb-set contract diffs reviewed" — the label-set diff is the four `EventKind`s and the verb-set diff nil; "a resume between N and N+1 reproduces the hash sequence" — at 5,000/1,000/500 ms with `pending` present at every encode and a recompute control diverging at each (M3.2); "an over-subscribed roster refuses by name" — eight command cores on a side of twelve under a lowered pool, before the first write, and twelve a side admitted at the stock constants where the gate cannot refuse (M3.5); "three injected faults drop a unit to the ROM" — three `mind_fault` lines and one `mind_dropped` carrying the fault and the budget, the fourth think the native stand-in at Class 0's row with one slot (M3.4); "`destroy_core` drops one at its next landing" — at three cadences on a doctored hull in M3.4 and on the shipped line tank from M3.6, the in-flight think landed as thought and continuous orders holding; the italics: the seal keyed `(unit, sortie)` with the id minted at the seal and carried by `MatchRecord.minds`, one `sortie_closed` per unit before the end's line or at destruction, a re-seat after a release asserted not to re-seal, a changed `MIND_ABI` refused by name on a reflex seal too; "both harness lanes hold their lenses" held at every sub-cut with the one declared re-bless of the ledger-derived fields in M3.6 (the ci window lens marginal on the blessing host throughout); the cut's record is 17b §3, row M3.
- **§6 and D-UM-22, D-UM-23 (the write moment), §8 "Faults" (the fault's moment and the think's substep hash on its lines), §9 "The pool" (the process-wide pool), §13 "The snapshot" (`awaiting`; the await on the encode path), §9 "The classes" (`mind_class` off the seal; `rated` on the terms)**: as built (17b cut M4.1, 2026-10-07, `c9d3b7ae`; the M4 row's finding (1): D-UM-22 and the substep hash cannot both be read literally — the pending answer is folded whole at every substep's end, while D-UM-22 ran a single-substep wake's batch after egress — so the plan ruled one write moment both paths share). **The write moment**: a think no longer runs its runner at its millisecond. `Arena::collect_think` freezes the job (`CollectedThink`: the view, the memory, the card, the row's profile, the hull's own runner), writes the think's FACTS on the row (`pending` with this `think_seq`, `t_ms`, the three epochs and an empty answer), names the hull in `Arena.awaiting`, advances `think_seq` and schedules the landing and the next think as before; the substep's record stamps every think it collected with that substep's own end hash (`CollectedThink.substep_hash`, where the plan said `substep_index`: `substep_hashes` is cleared at each step's head). `Arena::settle_collected` writes the answer at ONE sim-time moment on every path — after everything the think's substep's pulse journalled and before anything of the next: the head of the next substep inside `Arena::step` (`settle_inline`, the runner on the task's thread inside its unwind boundary and the stall watchdog, counted in the wake), the head of the next `step` for a caller that never delivered, or the task's delivery between pulses (`Arena::deliver_answers`, the same position, nothing being journalled between phase 5 and the next phase 1). So the per-substep hash sequence and the tape are one function of sim time whichever path ran the think, the think's substep folds the facts and an empty answer, and the fold code is unchanged. **The fault's moment**: a fault lands nothing, puts the drained notices and the range memory back, counts the strike, retracts the landing the collection scheduled and journals `mind_fault` (and on the dropping strike `mind_dropped`) under the THINK'S OWN substep's end hash at the think's own `t_ms`, written at the next substep's head — the tape is no longer `t_ms`-monotonic across those lines (declared). A think collected in the substep that ends the match is never run (nothing is written after the end); an answer whose row no longer awaits it — a wreck, a possess, a release — is dropped and counted. **`awaiting`**: "answered" is a scratch set beside the rows — never hashed, never carried, laid down empty at decode — because an empty memory is a legal clean answer, so `PendingThink` keeps M3.2's shape and `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` did not move here; `Snapshot::encode` refuses an arena with a hull in it as `SnapshotError::Awaiting { units }`, by name, and `mind_land` never lands an unwritten answer. `StepOutput.thinks` carries a pulse's unwritten thinks out as clones; `Arena::step_settled` is `step` with them run inline and delivered, which every rig that hashes, encodes or reads a mind row between pulses drives. **The pool** (`minds/pool.rs`): `MindPool`, `[tacsim.minds].workers` (stock 2, 1 to 32 by name, never carried) pure-std threads `tacsim-mind-N` over one queue of batches under one mutex and a condvar, first-in first-out per batch, a batch's jobs taken and answered in job order (`BatchHandle::wait`), `MindPool::shared` sized once a process; a worker catches a runner panic only to keep its thread, and the batch answers `BatchFailed` naming the job. **The await on the encode path**: the arena task submits a pulse's thinks after `publish` and awaits the batch through `tokio::task::spawn_blocking` at the next pulse's head and past the snapshot's due check (`snapshot_period_due`), before the encode — never right after the submit, which would serialise every batch, and through `spawn_blocking` because tokio's paused clock auto-advances while the runtime idles on a std thread; the wait is logged and summed as `pool_wait_us`, off the watchdog's clock. **A panic voids**: a failed batch, a stall and a runner panic inside `step` end the match `Void` — the first two through the library's own stop (`void_now`, the unwritten thinks discarded first), the third at the unwind boundary — never a fault (the sandbox catches its own). **`mind_class` off the seal**: `Arena::mind_class` is `min(UnitState::seal.class, the core's class now)`, `Zero` when dropped or coreless; `SealTerms` gains `rated` (`STOCK_RATED_CLASS`, I) beside `class_cap`, and `seal_for` writes `min(rated, core, cap)` — a cap alone could never reach Class III, the rating being I; the arena-wide `rated_class` and `set_rated_class_for_tests` are deleted. The one re-bless, declared: the pins over matches that carry a mind moved once (`M2_CROSS_PLATFORM_PINS`' substep and ledger digests, each row's stop millisecond and final state hash unmoved; the twin's `COVERAGE` and the pooled record's count lines, 10,566 → 10,562 thinks, the thinks of an ending substep never run); no production pin moved.
- **§18 "The lane", the CPU lens and "The fast path has one trigger" (where it is read), §9 "The pool" (admission counts every hull)**: as built (17b cut M4.2, 2026-10-08, `9be0b463`): `tacsim_harness_arena_minds` runs FOUR variants, fill first, where §18 names three — `fill_authored` (thirty-six Class I hulls a side on `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE` and the rest the ROM, at the authored reach: the production shape, and the variant the decision reads), `stock_ci` and `stock_authored` (every hull sealed `class_cap: Zero` on the native engine, so the ROM: the stock variants run no Rhai and their ops are nought by construction), and `ceiling_authored` (two `heavy_mk1` a side — the one chassis on the command core, every other cohort hull riding the Class II line core — rated and capped III on the scripted engine and flown by the calibrated load mind `tests/support/load_iii.rhai`, 4,397 ops plus 20 fee, its seal's `mind` and `source_sha256` written after custody; the two heavies stand deepest in each side's own rear, because the first run lost one at the line's edge to a turret hit whose `degrade_core` dropped it to Class II, where the load mind faults `ops`). **The ROM counted**: admission counts every hull, the ROM at 400 ops a second (§9), so at 300 units the 200,000 pool admits 36 a side at Class I and 2 at Class III (`admitted_class`, `load_of`, `admit_side`, pinned with the 37th's and the 3rd's exact `PoolRefusal` figures). The jitter is `TACSIM_MINDS_JITTER_MS` 250 on a `TACSIM_MINDS_JITTER_PER_MILLE` 250 fraction — one substep late, ON the lattice — where §18 wrote 60: a 60 ms remainder substep costs motion, the index, the due loop and the hash, none of it think time, and leaves three substeps in windows blessed on two; the window lens is asserted over the comparable windows (the lattice's two substeps, inline think time inside `Wake.ms`), the others printed beside. Faults are asserted nought on EVERY variant (§18: the stock variants). The verdict line `harness_minds_verdict` carries two words: `held=` (exactly the plan's asserts) and `sound=` (the lane's integrity, never a decision input; a `sound=no` run is re-run), with one panic at the end under `TACSIM_ASSERT`. **The CPU lens ruling (the programme lead's)**: asserted as the pool's ops per window over the host's measured ops per microsecond on Standard Doctrine over a real Class I view (200 thinks, measured once a run), times 1.25 — so it bounds per-window variance against the host's own rate, never interpreter speed; `[tacsim.minds].cpu_lens_ms` (§18's 5) is printed beside and never asserted. The measured rate (budget, ops plus fee): 9.380 per µs p50 on the build container at this cut (ops alone 8.761), 9.380 to 9.949 on the VPS (the decision runs' lens sentences), against §18's 19.5 to 24.1 on mind-shaped code (17b §2). **The trigger read on the fourth variant**: §18's trigger is read off the FILL variant's verdict over three idle-host weekly runs (`held=yes` three times keeps `scripted`, any counted `held=no` is `native`), not off `minds_stock_authored` — the stock variants fly no Rhai and cannot read it; a `stock_ci` miss was to be the owner's STOP before M4.5 moved the CI lane onto minds (waived by the owner, 2026-10-08). `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE` landed `scripted`, the candidate under test, with an empty decision log; the decision is the entry below. Reported, never asserted: `view_us` (forty wakes), think µs (Rhai and native), `pool_wait_us` (here the batch's wall latency with no pulse gap, an upper bound on the production blocked time), the `landings` counter, `inline_thinks`/`pooled_thinks` derived per wake. The deterministic block per variant (`harness-minds-<variant>.json`) is compared by the delta once the baseline carries a `minds` object (M4.5's one bless).
- **§12 "The flow" and §6 (the flip), D-UM-37 (`fill_class` at both gates), §12 "Possess and act-as" (the held order cleared at suspend; the Withdraw seed), D-UM-49 and §1 (the three readers off a cached doctrine state; the one machine flag), §13 (the stock source with no store; `SealEngine::Reflex` kept for records), §22 M4 (the production pins re-pointed and re-blessed once)**: as built (17b cut M4.3, 2026-10-08, `fbf23905`; doc 16 §11's M4.3 entry holds the fourteen divergences, the counted coverage, the seed, the landing latency and Gate B's lines verbatim). **What moved at the flip**: `match_::start` and `@arena/start` check every unseated hull out `Controller::Mind` and seal EVERY hull — the fill's and a seated pilot's alike — with Standard Doctrine at `class = min(STOCK_RATED_CLASS, the core, [tacsim.minds].fill_class)` on `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE` (`scripted` at the flip, `native` since the fill decision below), so a release hands any hull to a mind of the same class on the same engine and adds no unadmitted load; `SealEngine::Reflex` is decode-only from here (every M3 record carries it; kept through 17b cut M4.6c, D8 below). **`fill_controller` retired**: a config naming it refuses the boot by serde's unknown field; the bot rig's `arena_config_with_fill` is gone (the shipped fill IS the conditioned T1); `fill_class` is `0`, `I`, `II` or `III` by word, default `I`. **`fill_class` at both gates**: `pool::admitted_class(chosen, seated, fill_class)` counts an unseated hull at `fill_class` and a seated one at nothing (M5's gap, declared); at `SIM UNIT` the side's unclaimed pods under the AI fill ride `spoken`, and a choice that adds nothing is never refused, so `would think 0` is never said; twenty-four at Class I speak for 36,000 of the 476,000 pool; the two lobby call-offs the flip made reachable read short (118 and 97 columns). **The Withdraw seed and seated hulls at `fill_class`**: every `Released` hands its hull to a mind — the dormant row a possess left, or a fresh one — seeded `state = Withdraw, ordered = false` with the six Q.7/E.4 keys cleared as `ReflexMemory::adopt` cleared them (`seed_withdraw`; a memory of another shape becomes the stock seed, declared for M5's authored minds); `adopt_mind` seeds on `Some(Withdraw)` alone; the stock memory's fifteenth key `ordered` gates the restoration edge, so a released hull is left to Q.7's rally as the reflex left it (`STANDARD_SHA256` `8b3ec78c…` → `102cc7d2…`, the mind pins' second declared move). **The held order cleared at suspend**: `suspend_mind` clears the dormant row's order — an order given to the mind is not the pilot's and does not outlive the seat. **The cached doctrine state**: `MindState.doctrine_state` (`StockMemory.state` as the row's last landed memory decodes it, `Startup` for an empty or ill-shaped one; never hashed, never carried, re-derived at decode, refreshed at every write of the memory), read by the three readers — through `Arena::machine_state` for the leader's `doing:` histogram (a state word, never the memory: D-UM-49) and the Y.4 catch-up (`MachineWords::of_mind`; the sentence byte-equal), and directly (`any_mind_approaching`) for the tempo's `ai_approaching` (Alert for a mind in `Approach`, one substep after the reflex's tick would have raised it) — and gated on Standard Doctrine by the seal's doctrine bit from 17b cut M4.6c (below). **The one machine flag**: the observer prints `m` for every `is_machine()` hull, `n` retired (the renderers never knew `n`, so a mind read `pilot` until this cut). **The stock source with no store**: `minds::source_lookup` answers the compiled-in Standard Doctrine for its own digest before the store at both refusals (`resume::refuse_unrunnable_seals`, `arena_bridge::sealed_sources`), so a world whose store is off runs and resumes every production seal. **The pins re-pointed**: the production pins fielded `Controller::Reflex` explicitly, so they were re-pointed onto the production shape (`Muster::fill`, `SealTerms::production_fixture`) and re-blessed once with the reasons (the controller byte, `MindThink`/`MindLand` for `ReflexTick`, item 3c's rows in item 3's place, the seal digests, the `mind_sealed` source): the (j) field `0x18df_dd94_73aa_5a36` → `0x692d_5b0e_3c04_93a6` and `0x5689_35a8_5830_609d` → `0xd243_f6a1_30c0_00c7`, `DUEL_HASH_AT_HEAD` `0xd25d_39ba_9b04_7064` → `0x65eb_692a_b7a8_a72d`, `M1B0_J_LINES` 61 → 50 and `M1B0_DUEL_LINES` 34 re-recorded; The residual divergences named there: `range_round`, `line` (STOP J), `fallback`, `support`, `order_end` (re-pinned to `ignore_until_ms`) and one margin edge sub-quantum on T1.
- **§17 and §22 M4 (every other fixture, the twin and the harness onto minds; the counters), §3 (STOP L)**: as built (17b cut M4.5 in three commits, landed in the order M4.5a `4bbcbdc5` 2026-10-08, M4.5c `53457605` and M4.5b `f19a88fb` 2026-10-09, on the owner's rulings of 2026-10-08 and 2026-10-09). **M4.5a**: every Reflex-fielding fixture outside the harness flown by a mind (port 115, motor 14, mirrored 4, deleted 4, keep 13, rename 8, STOP 8); `ReflexCounters` renamed `MotorCounters` (`arena/motor.rs`, no alias) and `StepOutput.reflex` `motor`; `Muster::reflex` gone; the twin's T0 column retired — T1 against T1 over fifteen seeds, `M1B5_POOLED_RECORD` (67 lines) replaced by `M4_5_POOLED_RECORD` (21; red 13, blue 2), §17's Gate B Wilson interval and draw bound retired (a mirror pool scores a half by construction; its last reading is the flip's, above), the shadow's `support`, `order_hint`, `range_round`, `plot_cell` and `slot_starved` counters retired. Recorded production consequences: a mind's B-41/AA.1 end raises no `order_expired` until the order's TTL (the commander is not told); a mind's `ORDER` release leaves its motor row to its next think, where the reflex forgot goal and route; Q17's `Goal(Hold)` departure extends to the cornered Withdraw stand. **M4.5c**: the owner's two rulings on STOP L (the row cut seats the hull's lock and its standing order's contact, then keeps every contact not known friendly before its friends, the kept rows read nearest-first) are written into §3, §9 and D-UM-4 as amended 2026-10-09 and not repeated here; the one pin they moved is the twin's `COVERAGE.lance`, (5,878, 156) → (6,099, 145). **M4.5b**: every harness lane fielded at the ROM (`Controller::Mind` sealed at Class 0 on the fixture terms); **the three counters retired** — `ticks`, `in_state` and `ordered`, the reflex's own (`traces` followed with `reflex_has_line` in M4.6c); the report's `reflex` object is `motor` (nine counters, total and peak per pulse) and the state histogram `minds.states_at_end` (seven leaves off `Arena::machine_state`), so the compared leaves are 106 where they were 109; the lance lane's W8 fact pins re-pinned from its fight at the ROM (17b §2); the span read off the minds' live orders joined to the tape (STOP C, harness-only until M4.6c); the baseline blessed ONCE with the four minds blocks, on Linux (`arena.linux.json`, the stale `arena.windows.json` removed) under the owner's ruling of 2026-10-08, the timing gate dormant until a blessing host re-blessed it (the VPS, 16c T-109, below). **`ReflexConfig` kept**: the struct and `[tacsim.arena.reflex]` keep their names; `decide_ms` is the motor's tick cadence, the path budgets are the motor's and the doctrine figures feed the stock card.
- **§18 "The fast path has one trigger" and §22 M4 (the fill's engine)**: as decided (the fill decision, 2026-10-09, its own commit after 17b cut M4.5 and before M4.6's code; doc 16 §11 holds the full entry and the three lines verbatim, 17b §2 the baseline row). 17b cut M4.2 read §18's trigger off the minds lane's fill variant over three idle-host weekly runs on ALIEN18 (`held=yes` three times keeping the Rhai stock mind, any `held=no` promoting T1); the owner's three runs at `f19a88fb` all read `held=no sound=yes` (per-wake p99 17.297, 20.750 and 17.817 ms against 12; run 2's window p99 20.750 against 20; the CPU lens held on each), so `PRODUCTION_FILL_ENGINE` is `native`: the AI fill and every seated hull fly T1 at `fill_class`, the Rhai Standard Doctrine stays the exact reference held to it by Gate A and the twin, and a scripted seal still runs it. The VPS's runs (`held=no` on every lens, the CPU lens included) and both hosts' no-jitter diagnostics are recorded as context, not counted. The production hash moved for the seal digests alone (the engine folds into a seal's digest); every pinned tape is byte-equal but for its substep hashes.
- **§17 and D-UM-3 (what outlives the reflex controller)**: as built (17b cut M4.6a, 2026-10-09, `c0f2b6af`; a refactor that moved nothing hashed, carried, journalled, counted or printed — the harness delta equal on every block): `arena/doctrine.rs` holds the survivors — `MachineState` (the reflex's `ReflexState` renamed, its six words and code bytes unchanged; `Unknown` is M4.6c's), `STAND_TICKS` (`REFLEX_STAND_TICKS` renamed), the exposure, screen and support constants, `Ranges`, `stand_off_want`, `arc_between` and the `SEALED_*` identity (moved from `match_/seal.rs` and re-exported there, so the doctrine bit can read it inside the arena); `apply_machine_intents` and `Landed` moved to `mind.rs`, `hostile_in_weapons_range` and `health_permille` (`reflex_health_permille` renamed) to `state.rs`; `MatchMirror.minded` deleted (written, never read); the old public paths dropped, not aliased (no user anywhere). The two pins the plan feared orphaned — the reflex's e1 range table and d2's heat rows — held row for row off the stock mind's view and live on as `stock_mind.rs`' `e1_the_stock_tanks_three_rows_of_the_range_table_off_the_view` and `d2_a_weapon_that_makes_no_heat_never_holds_the_mind_back`.
- **§3 and D-UM-4 (the view's `immobile`: STOP B), §6 and D-UM-15 (the arena ends a `Support` whose charge left: STOP E), §17 and §22 M4 (the seven STOPs carried to M5), the help as written; and (17b cut M4.6c) §2, §4 and §17 (the reflex controller deleted; `NotMachine`), §13 (the seal's doctrine bit), D-UM-15 (STOP C's issuer)**: as built (17b cut M4.6b, 2026-10-10, under the owner's rulings of 2026-10-09; the reflex still compiles, `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT`, `FRAME_VERSION` and `STANDARD_SHA256` unmoved, the harness delta equal on every block). **STOP B ("power up, then park")**: `Arena::mind_view` reads `immobile = frame.immobile || (no authored speed && power Online)` — the one view field that is not the keyframe's (§3 and D-UM-4 amended above); the pilot's frame, `Frame.immobile` and the keyframe keep `limits.motion_disabled` alone; no field, no Rhai edit. A cold `target_mk1` reads mobile while `Off` and `Starting`, so its first think takes the power arm (`STARTUP`, landing at 439 ms and online at 939 on the rig's 500 ms startup), and its first think online reads `immobile` with no weapon rows — the `Disabled` edge — and every think after emits nothing: the help's "It powers up and stands where its pod put it" and `target_mk1`'s "comes online and a thermal sight reads it hot" stay true (its "a target still turns in place" is the hull's capability, which the frame keeps; the mind flying it never turns it, as the reflex, which parked it in `Patrol` with no goal and no sight, never did). The residues ported onto minds, every claim kept but the ruling's re-pin: `tests/reflex.rs`' `o3_an_inert_target_starts_up_and_searches_nothing` and `o3_an_inert_target_is_the_same_at_three_cadences` as `t1_an_inert_target_starts_up_and_searches_nothing` and `t1_an_inert_target_is_the_same_at_three_cadences` (parked `Patrol` → `Disabled`; `Power::Online`, one intent and one `startup` line HOLD under this form), `tests/weapons.rs`' `range_arena` on `Controller::Mind` (every claim unchanged, no re-pin), and the three non-range residues with their original claims (17b's critic G4): `aa1_a_hull_authored_with_no_speed_engages_the_same_way` → `t1_a_hull_authored_with_no_speed_engages_the_same_way`, `z2_a_supporter_that_cannot_close_fights_where_it_stands_past_its_leash` → `t1_a_supporter_that_cannot_close_fights_where_it_stands_past_its_leash`, and `tests/deliberation.rs`' `e1_an_ordered_close_caps_the_squad_at_the_pilots_own_close` legs 2-3 on `field`'s minds. The pins that discriminate STOP B are the four `tests/reflex.rs` ports `t1_an_inert_target_starts_up_and_searches_nothing`, `t1_an_inert_target_is_the_same_at_three_cadences`, `t1_a_hull_authored_with_no_speed_engages_the_same_way` and `t1_a_supporter_that_cannot_close_fights_where_it_stands_past_its_leash` (each fails with the ruling's line disabled) and the in-source `m4_6b_a_hull_authored_with_no_speed_reads_immobile_on_the_view_once_online`; `tests/deliberation.rs`' e1 legs 2-3 and `tests/weapons.rs`' range tests pass either way. A hull authored with no speed is, once online, also handed no heading by the stock mind's helm half (which withholds every goal and heading from an `immobile` view), though it could turn in place: an armed zero-speed mind holds its opening heading where the reflex turned it onto the plot — no shipped armed hull has zero speed, and no asserted claim moved. **STOP E ("the arena ends it")**: at the supporter's landing, beside and after `expire_mind_order`, `Arena::end_mind_support_whose_charge_left` ends a `Support { of: Some(of) }` whose row `of` in the supporter's own contact store holds a unit no longer in `Arena::units` — the order cleared, `orders_expired` counted, ONE `order_expired` line of the reflex's exact shape (`OrderExpired { unit, squad, objective: support }`) under the landing's lazy hash, the squad marked; the filter runs first and `event_hash()` is taken only once it passes (taken unconditionally it would fill the landing's `Pending` slot and move every later line's hash in the substep), so a match where no charge leaves folds and journals what it did. A wreck, or a charge the supporter's own row reads dead, ends nothing here (the mind stops obeying: `order_end`, §17). `z2_a_support_whose_charge_leaves_ends_at_one_millisecond` → `t1_a_support_whose_charge_leaves_ends_at_one_millisecond`, its moment re-pinned from the supporter's first reflex TICK after the loss (`(10,000, 10,000 + decide_ms]`) to its first mind LANDING after it (exactly the first landing past 10 s, read off the kept thinks — the think at 10,004 plus a 250 ms substep: 10,254), every other claim kept. **STOP E's two residuals**: (1) a `Support` landed with `of: None` — the supporter did not hold its charge classified when the order landed (`rekey_order`) — names no row and is NOT ended when its charge leaves (it stands blind until `expires_ms`), where the reflex's order named the object; closing it needs the charge's `ObjId` on the row, a decision-bearing (so hashed) field outside the ruling; (2) the end needs the supporter's row for its charge still in its store at the landing, so a supporter blind to its charge for a contact TTL when the charge leaves is not ended and stands blind until `expires_ms` — reachable at the stock figures (the blind stand of `t1_a_support_keeps_station_and_stands_blind`); a class whose `think_ms` exceeds the TTL misses it the same way; latent in production, where no path removes a unit from the match; the remedy is residual (1)'s, the charge's `ObjId` on the order (decision state: hashed and carried), carried to M5. The end keeps the arena right for any future leave and for the test fixtures that `retain`. **The seven STOPs carried to M5 (residues deleted, each loss and its remaining mirror)**: **A** (no match mode on the view; no production think hands the stock mind's Hold arms the objective) — the O.1 section's seven `o1_*` tests and `ae3_a_released_drone_in_a_hold_match_drives_to_the_spot_and_holds_nothing`: in a Hold match no mind drives to the spot, parks inside the square, retries a sealed spot, fights from inside the square or waits on it without a line, an `Advance` into the square is not ended on arrival, and a released drone does not drive to the spot (the last integration pin of the Hold retry with them; the field's snapshot carry stays pinned in `tests/arena.rs`); mirror: none — no production think reaches the Hold arms; what stays pinned beside them is `stock_mind.rs`' `m1a6_the_hold_arms_drive_for_the_spot_park_on_it_and_retry_it` and `m1a6_an_advance_into_the_square_ends_and_an_approach_inside_it_stays` (the kernels on hand-built holds), `hash.rs`' `o1_the_hash_folds_the_mode_the_hold_clock_and_the_hold_retry` (the fold), `state.rs`' `ae3_a_drone_holds_nothing_and_contests_nothing` (a drone alone holds nothing), and, beside them but pinning nothing, the report-only Hold lane (it asserts nothing and never reaches the Hold arms); **D** (`hint()` answers `None` for a `Support`) — `z2_a_support_keeps_station_and_prefers_its_charges_target`: a supporter does not prefer what its charge is fighting (a pilot's lock, a machine's target) and takes its own score's; mirrors: `tests/minds.rs`' `l2_a_self_charged_support_fights_on_doctrine` and its twin and Rhai siblings (the `of == self` half), `t1_a_support_keeps_station_and_stands_blind` and `stock_mind.rs`' `m1a6_a_supporter_stands_blind_rejoins_off_the_leash_and_keeps_station` (station and stand) — the designation half has NO mirror (the twin's `support` counter the residue's doc named was retired in 17b cut M4.5a); **F** (`resolve_place` stages no notice to a mind) — `i3_a_placed_machine_re_plans`: after a staff `PLACE` a mind under `Advance` searches nothing new and stands where staff put it until its goal next changes; mirror: `motor.rs`' `i3_forget_route_keeps_the_decision_and_drops_the_path`; **G** (nothing forgets a mind wreck's motor row) — `a_wreck_and_a_disabled_tank_order_nothing_at_all`: a mind's wreck keeps its motor goal and route as its last tick left them (folded by hash item 3b; no pin moves), and the hand-set `disabled = true` half is reached only by the production route; mirror: `t1_a_wreck_thinks_nothing_and_an_inert_hull_reads_disabled`; **I** (no AE.2 `stood` guard on a mind) — `tests/extensions.rs`' `ae2_a_machine_that_climbs_after_firing_is_not_taught_stop_i`: a mind that fires from behind a ridge and then climbs the crest is still taught `no line` by the blocked miss; mirror: none — case (2), its control, stays on the mind in `ae2_the_round_is_traced_from_where_it_left`, and the twin's `no_line` counter counts every think that read a `blocked`/`cover` miss notice (nought over the pinned pool), not the climb; **J** (the `line` divergence: a mind traces to the plot's cell) — `e4_t42_a_round_stopped_by_the_ground_re_plans_the_machine`: a round stopped by the ground does not re-plan a mind to `Approach` with its goal on the plot's cell when the plot's cell has a line — it re-engages in the same think; mirrors: `stock_mind.rs`' `m1a6_a_blocked_miss_notice_turns_engage_into_approach`, `minds/content.rs`' `m2_mirror_a_blocked_miss_notice_turns_engage_into_approach`, the twin's `line` counter; **K** (a mind's hunt cell is never fanned in its own memory) — `tests/shipped_scale.rs`' `e3_two_machines_of_one_side_cross_the_ford_without_stopping_each_other`: two minds of one side hunting together judge arrival at the first's cell and meet head-on at the far zone inside four minutes; mirrors: `tests/motor.rs`' `m1a4_the_fan_keeps_two_same_side_goals_apart_in_a_far_zone`, `motor.rs`' `ai1_the_hunters_of_one_side_are_handed_clear_cells`, the twin's `fan` counter. Fourteen tests deleted with the helpers only they called; no literal pin rode on any. **The help over-promises A and D since 17b cut M4.3 — recorded, not edited (the owner: M5 restores)**: for A, `docs/help/simulator.md`'s Hold paragraph ("The machines play it too: with nothing to fight they drive to the spot, stop anywhere inside the square, and fight from there") and `content/units/drone_mk1.json`'s note ("in a Hold match drives to the spot but holds nothing"; the pack untouched, under its pinned digest); for D, `docs/help/cockpit.md`'s `ORDER 1 SUPPORT RA` row ("fires first on what RA is fighting") and support paragraph ("shoots what that tank shoots … fires first on whatever that tank is fighting"), and the text's producer, `match_/cockpit.rs`' `order_ack` — both of its `Support` arms, the plain ("… fires first on what {charge} is fighting") and the HOLD FIRE ("… locks what {charge} is fighting but fires nothing") — with every pin of that text, all as written: in source, `match_/cockpit.rs`' `z2_the_acknowledgements_say_what_the_roles_and_the_release_mean` (both arms; `order_ack`'s doc quotes them too), `tests/cockpit.rs`' `z2_the_lead_screens_supports_and_releases_and_every_callsign_refusal_is_one_line` and `crates/mushd/tests/sim_solo.rs`' `y1_a_lone_pilot_orders_their_wingman` (the plain arm, "… and fires first on what RA is fighting"). And `docs/help/cockpit.md`'s support-end sentence ("If the tank leaves the match or its supporters see it destroyed, the order ends for them there and then") over-promises too — recorded, unedited: a charge that leaves is not ended under STOP E's residuals (1) and (2) (latent), and a charge seen destroyed ends nothing on the row (`order_end`: the mind stops obeying, the arena keeps the order standing). **A known stale comment**: `content/minds/standard.rhai`'s header still names `ReflexState::code` and `ReflexState::ALL` (17b cut M4.6a renamed the enum `arena::doctrine::MachineState`); the script sits under the pinned `STANDARD_SHA256`, so it stays as written until the next reviewed digest move. **17b cut M4.6c — the deletion (2026-10-10, under the owner's rulings of 2026-10-09)**: `arena/reflex.rs` deleted whole with its module and re-exports (the old public paths `tacsim::arena::reflex::*` dropped, not aliased: no user anywhere); `Controller::Reflex` (code `1`, word `reflex`) gone — the mind keeps its byte `2`, `1` is retired and never reused, and `is_machine` is the mind alone; `DueKind::ReflexTick`, `Arena.reflex` and every reader of it (hold-fire, the release, the held-order read, the frame's order, the Order landing's other arm, the wreck's adoption, the `no line` feed with its `stood`, the three motor guards, the heading disengage, the catch-up's reflex arm), hash item 3 and **only** item 3b's guard (3b folds every motor row as it did, `hold_retry_ms` included), and `MotorCounters.traces` with `reflex_has_line`. **No hash, line or harness pin moved**: item 3 folded per row with no count prefix, so a match with no reflex row — every match since 17b cut M4.3 — folded no byte of it. `set_controller` moved to the mind seam with only its Pilot↔Mind arms, its seed parameter kept and typed `MachineState` (D7); `release_mind`, the `Mind → Reflex` transition, deleted. **`MachineState::Unknown`** (code 6, word `unknown`, last in `ALL`, now seven; codes 0–5 unmoved) and **`MachineState::DOCTRINE`**, the six states the doctrine writes — what `stock_mind::state_of` decodes over, so no memory decodes to `Unknown` (the stock mind's exhaustive match gives it `Startup`'s empty arm), and what the content pin compares `standard.rhai`'s header table against (the script untouched, `STANDARD_SHA256` unmoved); the leader's `doing:` histogram is seven wide (zero counts are not printed, so no prompt moved). **D14**: the catch-up sentence has no phrase for `unknown` — the three renderers open the word ("The machine was unknown under …") and the bot reads `Line::Other`; unreachable before M5. **The doctrine bit** (D3): `SealRef::doctrine`, derived by IDENTITY in `MindSeal::seal_ref()` — the seal's `mind`, `charter` and `line` are Standard Doctrine's (`SEALED_MIND`, `SEALED_CHARTER`, `SEALED_LINE`), never the engine (one mind on two engines) and never a `MindSeal` field (a field there would move every seal digest and so every mind hash) — `false` on `SealRef::none()`; carried, never folded. It gates the three readers of a mind's doctrine state: `Arena::machine_state` (a wreck or a disabled hull reads `Disabled` first; a mind whose seal is not doctrine reads `Unknown`), and through it the `doing:` histogram and the catch-up sentence; and the tempo's `ai_approaching` (`any_mind_approaching` reads the cache directly, gated the same way). Every production seal is Standard Doctrine before M5, so the bit reads `true` on every hull a match fields; the minds lane's Load III ceiling hulls, renamed after the seal, read `unknown` where they read `startup` — never `approach`, so the tempo did not move, and no compared leaf reads their state. **Carried to M5 (recorded; no behaviour moved)**: the bit's one ungated writer — a release's `Withdraw` seed (`Arena::adopt_mind` → `seed_withdraw`) writes the doctrine's state into the memory of any mind it hands the hull to, doctrine or not; unreachable in production (every seal is Standard Doctrine, and the Load III hulls are never released), so the two readers' sentences (`SealRef::doctrine`'s and `Arena::machine_state`'s) say a mind sealed on anything else keeps no doctrine state *of its own* in its memory; what a released non-doctrine mind is handed is left for M5, which fields other minds, to gate or to define. **STOP C (the owner's ruling)**: `MindState::order_issuer: Option<ObjId>` beside the order — written where the order lands, from the same `Order` the `ordered` line journals, and cleared wherever the order is (a release, an expiry, a possess, STOP E's end) — carried by the snapshot and **never folded** (`hash.rs`' never-folded list; `MindState`'s "every field is hashed" amended): the hint is re-keyed once, at write time, in the issuer's scope (D-UM-15), so the issuer steers nothing after the landing, as `Order.by` (D-UM-52). `Arena::live_orders_by_issuer` reads the mind rows, grouping exactly as the harness's tape join (a mind's controller, wrecks counted, `expires_ms > now`, `None` under the squad's commander), which the harness's `CommandFigures::of` now asserts wherever W8's figures are read, moving no figure. **Retired** by the ruling: `tests/arena.rs`' `the_hash_folds_the_issuer_and_not_the_spot` (the issuer is a record field beside a mind's order, and on a mind row `spot` and `by` are not stored at all), replaced by an in-source probe that the issuer is NOT folded and by the snapshot round trip that carries it; `tests/reflex.rs`' y2 ported onto minds through `IntentKind::Order` as `t1_live_orders_by_issuer_groups_per_issuer_and_the_machines_under_their_commander`, its four reads unchanged. **`Refusal::NotReflex` → `NotMachine`** (§4's rename; word `not_reflex` → `not_machine`): the cockpit's sentence ("That tank has a pilot in it.") unchanged; no tape carries the word (a refused order journals nothing) and a mind's buffer never receives it; the bot's contract and the legacy-handler note follow. **The format move**: `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 8 → 9, the plan's one move — the `reflex` section and two carried variants out, `SealRef::doctrine` and `MindState::order_issuer` in, the carried word `not_machine`; the `af1` shape pin walks the mind squad arena (the reflex squad fixture deleted), given before it is encoded one motor row (a mind's check-out lays none, where the reflex's adoption laid one per machine) and one squad order with an issuer LANDED through `apply_intent`'s Order arm, so it walks a motor row's nine fields, a standing order's `FrameOrder` and `order_issuer` as a number; re-blessed once (`5710d7c3…`, 442 lines → `202e7d4f…`, 509: 93 left, 160 arrived), and the eight literal-8 format asserts read 9. **Deploy**: the first reload onto this build voids every match running across it `aborted(restart)`, "the snapshot does not decode: snapshot format 8 is not 9"; nothing migrates. **D8, recorded**: `SealEngine::Reflex` stays decodable — every live record an M3.3–M4.2 build wrote for a running match carries it, and without the variant that record would fail to decode before its snapshot is refused by name — and two in-memory defaults still write it, unhashed and deciding nothing: `SealRef::none()` and `MindSeal::placeholder()`. The two pins D11 feared orphaned (the e1 range table, d2's rows) have their mirrors on the view since 17b cut M4.6a, so the reflex's in-source tests died with it and no claim with them; the decision commit's "a pre-decision match resumes on its scripted seals" holds until this cut's format 9, which voids every older snapshot (amended where it is said).
- **§18 (the lenses the minds lane asserts, and the host they are blessed on)**: as built (16c `T-108` and `T-109`, 2026-10-09, `417c101d` and `df078534` on `perf/vps-300`, merged after 17b cut M4.6 at `83a7709e`, 2026-10-10; doc 16 §11's T-109 entry holds the arena's side). The owner ruled that the wake and window lenses follow the production host, the conclav.io VPS (`srv1930616`, 4 vCPU AMD EPYC 7543P, 1.75 to 2 times slower a core than ALIEN18): §18's per-wake 12 ms is 20 on both cohorts, the CI window 22 (split from the per-wake lens) and the authored window 32; `arena.linux.json` is blessed on the VPS under its own host name, so the delta's timing gate is live there and the VPS is the blessing host. T-108: the minds lane asserts its per-wake lens over the UNJITTERED wakes only (`WakeLens`, split on the clock's own `late` flag), the jittered quarter's p99 printed beside (`wake_p99_other_ms`) — a jittered wake steps two substeps with its first lattice point's thinks inline, so the jittered quarter owned the all-wake p99 and even the all-native variants missed a lens calibrated on one substep; the inline path's time stays asserted by the window lens over the comparable windows, and a run with no unjittered wake does not hold. The fill decision's seven `minds.fill_authored` leaves were carried onto the VPS bless at the merge, with no re-bless.
- **§22 M4 (the gate as met, and what is owed)**: "`tacsim_harness_arena_minds` … with jittered wakes and the arena-task pool, which awaits any outstanding think batch before a snapshot is encoded" — built (17b cuts M4.1 and M4.2), with four variants for three; "AI fill flipped" — at 17b cut M4.3 on Rhai, on the native stock mind since the fill decision; "`Controller::Reflex`, `ReflexTick` and the decision half of `reflex.rs` deleted; `NotReflex` → `NotMachine`" — all of `reflex.rs` (17b cut M4.6c); "the deterministic block committed to the baseline" — once, with the four minds blocks, at 17b cut M4.5b, the fill block's seven leaves re-blessed by the fill decision and the thresholds by T-109 on the VPS, the delta reading every block equal at every commit since; "if the stock lane fails, the fast-path decision … recorded in doc 16 §11 before the flip" — the trigger was read on the fill variant, and the decision was recorded AFTER the flip (which ran on the candidate, `scripted`) and before 17b cut M4.6, in doc 16 §11's fill-decision entry. **"Every lens holds three times running on the blessing host through `scripts/harness-weekly.sh`" was RUN ON 2026-10-12 AND DID NOT HOLD** (three asserted runs on the idle VPS at `47bcb620`, 17b §2's row: one run of three held the minds lane whole, the authored cohort's window p99 27.5 to 33.0 ms against 32 — fill and ceiling 32.97 and 32.64 in run 1, one long stretch of `stock_authored` in run 2 — every other lane held three times and the delta read every block equal with the timing gate live; then at hash v2 `16320d7c` after 16c T-105 and T-104: the minds lane held 10 of 12 asserting runs and the last pass went whole, burst, whole — the letter of three running not met on this host, the lenses themselves holding with 15 to 30 per cent of margin whenever the box is itself; then at `9bbd2b4a` (2026-10-13, after 16c T-107a to T-107d and T-110a) every lens held three times running and the delta's timing guard tripped once at +20.1 against its 20 per cent bound with every block equal); what it asks is the four variants holding every lens three times running through `scripts/harness-weekly.sh` on the idle blessing host with the load printed — since T-109 the VPS (`srv1930616`) with the timing gate live — on the merged tree (`dd4483dd` or later: the native fill, T-108's unjittered per-wake lens, T-109's figures). Before those three no run of that shape existed: the owner's ALIEN18 runs of 2026-10-09 were the decision's (the old lenses, the scripted fill); the first asserted VPS runs (T-109, T-108) predate the native fill on that branch, their one remaining miss the scripted fill's window (33.093 ms against 32) and CPU (14.196 against 13.071); the merged tree's runs on the build container are report-only (all four `held=yes sound=yes` there, the fill's unjittered wake p99 13.959 ms, window 25.858; again in the M4.7 sweep on `dd4483dd`, 11.469 and 25.798 ms, CPU p99 1.781) — not the gate. M4 is landed except that gate; the web suite and the full sweep held on `dd4483dd` (the M4.7 sweep, 17b §3 row M4's Evidence (12) and (13)); the cut's record is 17b §3, row M4, and every residue it hands on is indexed in the next entry.

- **§12 "In the OOC sim lobby" (`SIM MIND`, `SIM AI MIND`), §9 "The pool" and "The sim", D-UM-36, D-UM-37 (the pool at the choice; seated hulls; the sentence), D-UM-49 (the holdings), §11 "Profiles" (the count at the sealed class) and §9 "The classes" (a class's rows)**: as built (17b cut M5.1, 2026-10-11, `4078b9dd`, on the owner's M5 rulings of 2026-10-10 — OQ-2, OQ-3, OQ-9, OQ-11 — and the orchestrator's lowering rule at its close). **The holdings seam** (`match_/holdings.rs`): `HeldMind { identity, rated, engine, presets, source }`, the `Holdings` trait (`held(w, character)` and a provided `sources()`, a declared departure: the installer must know the texts before any character is known), production `DoctrineOnly` — Standard Doctrine and its six presets for every character, keyed by the character who boards (`Slot.pilot`; Q-UM-13's default, confirmed by OQ-2) — registered as `HoldingsSeam`, `install_holdings` (each provider's sources into the store's RAM map through `store::install_mind_source`, split out of `put_mind_source`), `held_by`, `SealChoice { held, profiles, chose, substituted }`; `SealIdentity` (the five identity fields `seal_for` writes) in `match_/seal.rs`, and `seal::odds_are_lawful` lifted out of `seal_for` with its predicate unchanged. A test provider (`tests/support/holdings.rs`) holds two scripted fixture minds through the production seam: a new rated-I `Fixture I` (`tests/support/fixture_i.rhai`, `startup()` until online then `hold()`, digest `e2e7ac4d…1b28` pinned), always chosen on a `line_tank`, and the existing rated-III `Load III` for the over-class refusal alone. **The verbs**: `Selector::Mind`, the fourteenth; `SIM MIND [<mind> [PROFILE <p>[, <p>]…] | none]` and `SIM AI MIND [[red|blue] <mind> [PROFILE <p>] | none]` (read inside `SIM AI` before its number; `SIM AI`'s own refusals first and in its order), every reply to the actor alone and nothing announced; `none` clears, `@<rev>` is refused (no revisions before M6), profiles resolve case-folded and Line alone is stored as the empty set; staff, who hold nothing (D-UM-49), set a fill on Standard Doctrine's presets only. **The fit**, one rule for `SIM MIND` and `SIM UNIT`: over-class on a governed core (C-24, read off the chassis type's content-declared core, `core_of_type` beside `class_of_type`, so the pod emulates the chassis's core and nothing IC is read: R-9), the profile count at the class the mind will seal at (`min(rated, core, fill_class)`, Class 0 on a coreless chassis, which reads `This target_mk1 has no core and flies the ROM, which seals one profile at most.` and `Fits target_mk1 (ROM): …`), the card's pair on every chassis and the odds floor on an armed one (C-23), then the pool at the slot's own class; `SIM UNIT` re-fits a standing choice, so the later of the two is the one refused (§12); a change that lowers a slot's load is never refused, one that keeps or raises it is asked of the side after it (the orchestrator's ruling at the cut's close; unreachable at the stock constants). **The state**: `Slot.mind`, `Centre.fill_minds` (`FillChoice { team, by, choice }`, the setter recorded), each `#[serde(default)]` and skipped when empty; a change of a side's lead before the check-out clears a lead-set fill choice, staff's excepted. **The fog in `Selection`** (C-4, doc 17c §2.4): the board names a slot's mind to its own character and staff (`, mind Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line)`) and marks `, a mind` for everyone else, a side's fill row likewise for its lead (`  red fill: a mind`), nothing against an unchosen slot or fill and nothing from `Boarding` on; the bare reads per state (`Yours: none; a tank you leave flies Standard Doctrine on Line.`, a `Fits` line per held mind — `Fits line_tank (II): Standard Doctrine, up to two of Line, Sentry, Sniper, Skirmisher, Hunter or Rearguard.`, 115 columns at the longest chassis word — and the pool line, `Red's half of the centre's pool is 244,000 ops a second and 1,500 are spoken for.`; `…; selection is closed.` from `Boarding` on; `The fills are not shown once a match starts.` to everyone but the fill's setter and staff). **To the check-out**: `ready()` resolves every seated slot's choice and the side's fill choice against the seam (`SimError::MindNotHeld`, called off by slot and naming no mind), writing `Side.minds` (one `SealChoice` per fielded hull, `unchosen()` for the rest) and `Fielded.mind_class` for a chosen hull; the check-out still sealed Standard Doctrine on Line until 17b cut M5.2. **The pool**: `pool::admitted_class(chosen, fill_class)` admits every hull that chose no mind at `fill_class`, seated or not (M4's carried "seated hulls at the pool" discharged), and a chosen hull at its sealed class; `PoolRefusal` in OQ-11's words (`This tank would think {would} ops a second; its side's half of the centre's pool is {half} and {spoken} are spoken for.`, at most 118 columns reachable), `StartError::Pool { team, unit, callsign, refusal }` naming its hull in the long form (logged at `warn`), and the last `BOARD`er reading the call-off's short form. **The rider**: `[tacsim.minds].classes.*.rows` is validated 2 to 32 (M4's carried one-row class: a class now holds the seat's lock and its order's contact). The bot strips `, a mind` from a board row (`BoardPilot.mind`) and never types `SIM MIND`; `help simulator` gains the two selector rows and "Choosing a mind". No hash, format, frame, seal byte or tape line moved; the harness delta equal on every block.
- **§12 "Sealing" (the seal carries the choice; the one line before `STARTUP`), §11 "Profiles" (the sealed set and the active one) and D-UM-44 (a recorded switch), §13 "The snapshot" (`SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 10), §10 "The governor" and D-UM-41 (over-class), §11 and D-UM-43 (R-60: the floor only on an armed hull), §6 and D-UM-15 (STOP E's charge), §12 "Release and possess" (the sealed profile flies; the doctrine-gated seed; Q-UM-15)**: as built (17b cut M5.2, 2026-10-11, `73f85147`, with the orchestrator's S1 and D21 rulings of that day). **The seal**: `SealTerms.identity` and `SealTerms.profiles` (empty for the Line card alone, the card the first profile's), `rated` and `engine_for_minds` staying the terms' own and copied from the held mind at `match_::start`; `seal_for` writes the identity, validates every profile's card (its pair on every hull, the floor probe only on an armed hull: C-23), refuses `SealError::OverClass` when a governed core of Class I or more is below the mind's rating (C-24 as amended by S1, below), and writes `MindSeal.profiles: Vec<SealedProfile { name, card_digest }>` LAST under `skip_serializing_if`, empty exactly when the set is Line alone, so every default seal is the `5f505c8` record byte for byte (`m5_a_default_seal_digests_as_it_did_at_5f505c8`, nine digests read before any seal edit and pinned first). `StartError::MindOverClass` and (the review's) `StartError::CardRefused` call a match off by hull and name no profile, both unreachable through the lobby. **The sealed and the active profile**: `card::ProfileSet` (preset codes, `Copy`) on `SealRef.profiles`, never folded; `UnitState.card_profile: ActiveProfile { index, switched_ms }`, carried and folded as item 1's sub-item 20 only off its default (`state_hash` of the two-tank field pinned at its `5f505c8` value, `0x15a4_d2a7_9200_fae0`). **The switch** (C-20; D-UM-44's recorded intent at most once in ten seconds): `IntentKind::Profile { index }` on the helm, seat-only (`Mind`, `Machine` and `Cond` refused `channel`), continuous under its own key (`continuous_code` 5, so a heading or an order of the same pulse keeps its key), in its own arm of `apply_intent` before the power and motion gates (a cold or immobile hull switches), refused `Refusal::Profile` (word `profile`; `MIND refused. That profile cannot be switched to now.`) for no seal, an index past the set, the active profile or a landing inside `PROFILE_SWITCH_MS` (10,000) of the last switch; a landed switch writes a standing row's card and journals one `mind_card` line; no station function, so `API_VERSION` and `MIND_ABI` stand. **Release**: a new mind row is laid on the active profile's card (`MindState::fresh_with`, Line's byte for byte for a default seal), and the `Withdraw` seed is written only over a Standard Doctrine seal, so a non-Doctrine mind keeps its own memory (M4's carried ungated seed closed). **The check-out** reads `Side.minds` per hull (a missing entry seals exactly as before), installs a chosen held source in the store's RAM before its seal is used, and refuses a scripted seal whose source the store lacks before any write. **Q-UM-15** (OQ-5; C-7): `Centre.doctrine_only`, decided once at `SIM START` (both sides hold a claimed slot), substitutes a non-Doctrine choice at the seal — a pilot's by Standard Doctrine on Line, a lead's fill by Standard Doctrine at the lead's chosen preset when it is Doctrine's, else Line — with `MindSeal.fallback` `humans on the other side`, and `Humans on the other side: your fill and any released hull fly Standard Doctrine.` told to each affected pilot alone, once; fixture-only in M5. **The seal line** (C-18): `Sealed for this sortie: Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line), Class I; MIND reads it.` before `Good hunting.`, to each seated pilot whose slot chose (or was substituted) alone, through an audience naming that pilot (never the actor); widest 103 columns with the substitution clause. **STOP E's charge** (both M4 residuals closed): `MindState.order_charge`, written from a landed `Support { of }` and cleared with the order at a release, a possess and the arena's end, folded inside item 3c's `Support` arm alone; the arena ends a held `Support` whose charge has left `Arena::units`, so a `Support` landed with `of: None` and a supporter blind for a contact TTL are ended when the charge leaves; no fixture's hash moved. **The format move**: `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` 9 → 10, M5's one (`SealRef.profiles`, `UnitState.card_profile`, `MindState.order_charge`, the two variants); the `af1` shape pin's one move, `202e7d4f…` (509 lines) → `811a1ce79c8ae983ad8cce0c67b41765449341e76f0811c9596f3b14777d5611` (556), walking a mover sealed `[Sniper, Line]` and switched to profile 1, its motor row with a goal, a two-cell route, an avoided cell and a refused stop, and every objective's fields (D21; the index item below closed). The wire: `profile` in the bot's exhaustive `every_refusal`, the TUI's and the web's `REFUSAL_WORDS` and `notice_lines.json`. No production hash pin moved; the harness delta equal on every block.
- **§12 "Release and possess" (the board's word, the reconnect, the release acknowledgement) and D14 (the catch-up's `unknown`; the 17b cut M4.6b/c bullet above)**: as built (17b cut M5.3, 2026-10-11, `fb1c4691`; sentences only): one word for every mind, so nothing leaks the kind of a released hull's mind. The board's drop clause is `, flown by its mind` (`lobby::DROP_CLAUSE` through `lobby::board_standing_clause`; 19 columns, as wide as `, flown by doctrine`), an `EXIT` keeping `, left` (C-11); the reconnect line (`resume::mind_line`, was `doctrine_line`) `Red Tank 1 is flying on its mind; BOARD takes it back.`; the release acknowledgement `Released red-1 to its mind: it fights on its own until it is ordered again.` (75 columns) and `Released red-1 and red-2 to their minds: they fight on their own until they are ordered again.` (94); D14 closed — the machine state `unknown` (a mind not sealed on Standard Doctrine, fixture-only) reads `flying on its own mind` on line mode, the TUI and the web (`  The machine was flying on its own mind under no order.`, 56), `catchup_lines.json` gaining a tenth, hand-written case appended last, the wire word staying `unknown`. C-11a's audit: no other cockpit sentence changed. The bot's readers moved once, a declared reader change deployed with the server: `Standing::Doctrine` → `Standing::Mind`, `Line::Doctrine` → `Line::OnItsMind` (accepted at the close), the old reconnect line now `Line::Other`. Pinned by `m5_d14_the_catch_up_says_its_own_mind_in_three_renderers` (three renderers), `arena::catchup`'s `m5_d14_a_hull_not_on_doctrine_comes_back_flying_on_its_own_mind`, the bot's `m5_3_the_release_words_read_as_the_bot_reads_them` and `arena_lane`'s `m5_3_a_fresh_run_reads_its_row_flown_by_its_mind_and_boards_back` (the resume flow end to end). No hash, format, frame, seal, `af1`, golden or baseline moved.
- **§12 "The `MIND` verb" (line mode; `MIND PROFILE`, `MIND RULES`, `MIND TRACE`), D-UM-10 (the seventeenth selector, C-1), §3 and D-UM-4 (the keyframe's `mind` object, never the view)**: as built (17b cut M5.4, 2026-10-11, `51fd6ceb`, with the orchestrator's D-B1, D-B2 and D-B10 rulings at its close). `"mind"` is appended to `COCKPIT_VERBS` (16 → 17, after `DRIVE`; nineteen verbs with `BOARD` and `EXIT`), gated as every selector; `m`, `mi` and `min` move from no command to `MIND`, no kernel verb losing a prefix (`m5_mind_collides_with_no_registered_command`, a non-baseline `mind` stanza declaring it). `Frame.mind: Option<FrameMind { doctrine, profiles, active, class, core_class, switch_at_ms, off }>`, built in `frame_for` off the hull's `SealRef` with both classes read live (`Arena::mind_class`, `Arena::core_of`: a degraded core reads as it is), `switch_at_ms` absolute, compared by `Frame::same_as` (a switch on a quiet stationary hull sends exactly one frame), never copied into `MindView` (`PARITY` untouched); the keyframe key `mind` on a seated pilot's status frame alone (`null` with no seal; never the replay's or the watch's), `{ name, profiles, active, class, core, channels, next_switch_ms, off }`, `name` null for any mind but Standard Doctrine, so no other mind is named on the wire (the name of a non-Doctrine seal waits for the cut that seals one in production) — `FRAME_VERSION` held at 3, its policy gaining one sentence (a field added for the person in the seat is not a version); the canonical `cockpit_frame.json` and `view_frame.json` re-blessed once (+17 lines each, the `mind` object alone). Bare `MIND` is a block of at most seven lines (four today), each under 120 columns, read world-side off the held keyframe: `Mind: Standard Doctrine, sealed for this sortie; it thinks at Class I on a Class II core.`; `Profile: Sniper, 1 of 2 (also Line); MIND PROFILE Line switches, once in ten seconds.` (the set of one `Profile: Line, 1 of 1; no other is sealed to switch to.`, the in-window form with its countdown); `Channels: you hold helm, sensors and fire; the mind is dormant while you fly.` (OQ-8; the unarmed form names the missing weapons station); `Rules: none sealed; your own armings are under WEAPONSTATUS.` (OQ-4); the ROM forms keyed on the class the ROM rule reads (`…, off for the match: the hull is on the ROM.`; `…; with no core above Class 0, the hull is on the ROM.`; `… sealed for this sortie at Class 0 on a Class II core: the hull is on the ROM.` — D-B2, both kept by the ruling) and `This hull carries no seal.`; no ops figure, no seal id (C-14) and no card drawn (C-22). `MIND PROFILE <name>`: every local pre-check one line opening `MIND refused.`, composed through `order_refused` from the intent's own word as the arena's `profile` refusal is (not sealed, naming the sealed set; a word no preset answers, never echoed; already the profile; one switch in ten seconds with the countdown; no seal; no frame yet; a wreck), no power test, then the seat stages M5.2's intent on the helm (`Profile Sniper: the mind will fly it when you leave the seat.`); a routing refusal keeps every verb's `Refused: … .` (D-B9). `MIND RULES` is one line; `MIND TRACE refused. This core keeps no trace of its thinks yet.` until M6's ring. **The clock** (D-B1, ruled): the countdown and the window pre-check read the mirror's arena clock `MatchMirror::now_ms` (the arena's own clock, raised on every `Frames` message), not `MatchMirror.arena_ms` (the last ledger stamp, which lags a quiet match) as the design note named it. **A staff read-back follows a pilot's switch**: the world's mirror absorbs the seat's `mind_card` line, so `@mind/set` reads and edits the switched profile. D-B10 (ruled: left for M5): `Frame.mind` is built on every `frame_for`, a mind's view included, which discards it — about 450 to 540 ns a call on a release build, at most about 2.5 per cent of a wake's CPU p99 in the minds lanes — carried as a perf note to the arena-step work (16c `T-104`). The observer gate (`the_observer_has_no_path_into_a_pilot_feed`) now scans `frame_mind` too; the bot reads each pre-check as `Line::Refused { order: "MIND" }` and every other `MIND` line as a neutral line, and never types `MIND`. The fan-out lane's `panel_bytes_max` 7,931 → 8,093 (bound 16,384), `events_per_pulse_max` 288 unchanged; no hash, format, seal or baseline moved.
- **§12 "The `MIND` verb" on the TUI and the web (the block's place; C-6, C-22)**: as built (17b cut M5.5, 2026-10-11, `354e0ae8`; renderers only), on the orchestrator's decision of 2026-10-11 (the weapons pane's tail, not a fourth Right pane, which would cost the contacts pane two rows at both budget sizes): `render_cockpit_mind_tail` (TUI) and `mindLines` (web) draw four rows under the weapon and arming rows from the keyframe's `mind` object — `mind Standard Doctrine · Class I` (`mind: your own · Class I` for another mind; `|` for `·` on a terminal that is not UTF-8), `> Sniper   Line` (the flown profile marked), `dormant while you fly` (or `off for the match: on the ROM` for a dropped row or a live class of 0, matching line mode's ROM head), `you: helm sensors fire` — each at most 44 columns, in `(no weapon)`'s place on an unarmed hull; no pane, split, `COCKPIT_ROWS` or degrade change, so 16b cut AB.1's contact budget is untouched; the tail is clipped from the bottom and never the weapon rows (the canonical frame shows it whole at 120 × 34 and loses its channel row at 120 × 30; `heavy_mk1` with three guns armed shows none of it at 120 × 30); no countdown drawn (line mode's `MIND` prints it), no key bound, no card and no rule glyph (C-22: M6's); the replay and `@arena/watch` screens draw no tail. On the web each sealed profile the mind is not flying is a button that prefills `MIND PROFILE <name>` and sends nothing until Enter (C-6), the flown one plain text (the orchestrator's ruling at the review: a switch to it is refused). The four `tui_cockpit_*` goldens and `web_view_model.txt` re-blessed once, each diff read line by line; `m5_the_tui_and_the_web_draw_the_same_mind_tail` holds the two renderers' rows equal. No tacsim file, hash, format, `FRAME_VERSION` or sentence moved.
- **§22 M5's `Your mind` block of `SIM REPORT` (OQ-9 (ii)), §15 (the first hint, C-12; `help minds`) and §23's three lines of `help minds`, the help made true (OQ-6's text) and the M5.3 close's D9 (the remaining pilot-facing "doctrine" sentences)**: as built (17b cut M5.6, 2026-10-11, `46f6a796`). **The block**: `Report.mind: Option<MindBlock>` and the live `Fold.mind` (`#[serde(default)]`; `LIVE_FORMAT` stays 1); `match_::start` seeds each pilot's fold `.with_own_mind(OwnMind { chose, mind, profiles, substituted })` beside `.with_own_tank` (the orchestrator's decision: seeded at check-out, not a `profiles` key on `mind_sealed`, which could not say whether a slot chose); `lobby::slot_left` opens a span (an `EXIT`, a drop, a `QUIT`, a resume) and `slot_reclaimed` closes it, both on `MatchMirror::now_ms` (the clock ruling above; the one report fact not told from the tape or the check-out, 16b cut V.3's rule, declared), a span on a wreck dropped and a running one ended at the destruction; from the tape the pilot's own hull's `mind_card` lines by the card's own name (a staff edit reads `Sniper, edited (min_odds 70)`), its `mind_dropped` cause word alone (D17) and the shots fired inside a span with their hits and kills. Printed after the report's opening lines, only when the slot chose a mind or a span opened: `Your mind: Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line); switched to Line at 1:05.`, `  It flew your tank from 0:42, when you climbed out, to the end.` (three or more spans bounded, `N times: first …, last …`), `  Its shots: cannon 4, 2 of them hits; it destroyed nothing.`, `  It dropped to the ROM at 1:30: its core was destroyed.` — every line under 120 columns (widest 115); the pilot's own rounds, kills, `You fired …`, timeline and document counts and the staff fold unchanged. **The sixth first-time hint** (`HintPoint::Mind`): `Hint: SIM MIND Standard lets your tank's core fly the Registry's doctrine your way when you climb out.`, said by `match_::finish` once per account to each connected pilot of the match (§15's "by your rules" reworded, C-12; the second hint M6's, OQ-10 (a)). **`help minds`** (`docs/help/minds.md`, new, bundled into the bot) opens with the three lines §23 asks to stay true — `Your tank's core can fly it when you are not in the seat. SIM MIND chooses its mind; the match's start seals it.` / `With none chosen, a tank you leave flies Standard Doctrine on Line, the Registry's own; MIND reads it in the cockpit.` / `It flies from when you climb out to the end, or from a drop past the grace until the end or you board again.` — pinned to the code's values by `m5_help_minds_opens_with_three_true_lines` (since M5.7a also `help cockpit`'s copy of `MIND`'s four lines), and says the eleven `[M2]` card terms are carried inert (OQ-10 (e)) and promises no verb that does not exist. **The help made true** (OQ-6, the text in M5 and the behaviour in the residues cut after it): `help simulator`'s Hold paragraph (no machine drives for the key spot; they fight what they find), its `EXIT` and drop paragraphs, "Choosing a mind" and the report's block; `help cockpit`'s `ORDER` summary, the rule-language line, the `SUPPORT` row and paragraph (a supporter keeps station and fights what it finds there; the order ends when the charge leaves; one seen destroyed fights on its own — STOP D's designation half accepted under D-UM-15), its release and lapse paragraphs, a "Your tank's mind" section, the screen's mind rows and the profile click; `order_ack`'s two `Support` arms (`Ordered red-1 to support RA: it keeps within 150 m of RA and fights what it finds there.`); `drone_mk1`'s note (version 2 → 3, digest `8ea37961…`, was `1bfeca3a…`); the leader's system prompt and order tool (support keeps station and fights what it finds; the squad does not drive for the spot unless ordered to advance on it; "its own doctrine", the prompt being model-facing). **D9** (ruled at M5.3's close): `Squad red-1: your hold order has run out; back on their minds.` (was `back on doctrine.`), `ORDER`'s help `RELEASE hands them back to their own minds` and the report's `released red-1 to their minds.`, one word for every mind; "Standard Doctrine" stays the mind's name; - **§22 M5 (the gate as met)**: as built (17b cut M5.7a, 2026-10-12, `e4da2d7e`; this record is 17b cut M5.7b). "A line-mode transcript on the local rig of a pilot sealing a mind, exiting, and the tank flown by it" — `crates/mushd/tests/sim_solo.rs`' `m5_a_pilot_seals_a_mind_climbs_out_and_the_tank_is_flown_by_it`, the all-in-one `mushd` over real SSH, Ada at 120 × 40 against the AI fill: the bare `SIM MIND`, `Sentinel` refused as not held, `SIM MIND Standard PROFILE Sniper, Line`, her board row `, mind Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line)`, no `Humans on the other side` at `SIM START`, `BOARD` with `Sealed for this sortie: Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line), Class I; MIND reads it.` before `Good hunting.`, `MIND`'s four lines, `MIND PROFILE Hunter` refused, two arena seconds in the seat with `STATUS` reading `power off` just before a cold `EXIT`, and her report's `Your mind: Standard Doctrine (Sniper, Line).` / `  It flew your tank from 0:02, when you climbed out, to the end.` / `  It fired nothing.`; the store's tape holds her hull's `mind_sealed` card digest as Sniper's over the rig's doctrine (`6edd245e…`) and the fill's as Line's (`1a04f1f7…`, `M1B0_DUEL_LINES`' own), and her hull's one `startup` and `power_online` at or after the start of her exit's arena second (3,110 and 3,410 ms in the gate sweep's run; 2,904 and 3,204 ms in the run after the review fixes, inside the exit's own second, which the tape keeps no release line to order — there the `power off` read and no `Power online.` while she sat show the tank was cold at the `EXIT`), so only its mind started it; no sixteen-digit word in any byte Ada saw (`no_sixteen_digit_word`); about 15 s in debug, asserted under 150 s, not ignored. "Goldens and the bot parser updated" — the canonical cockpit fixtures (M5.4), the four `tui_cockpit_*` goldens and `web_view_model.txt` (M5.5), each re-blessed once, and the hand-written `notice_lines.json` (M5.2) and `catchup_lines.json` (M5.3); `cargo test -p mush-bot` green on every new sentence, `SIM MIND` and `MIND` in `arena_e2e.rs`' `forbidden`. "Fog asserted" — `crates/mushd/tests/observer_rule.rs`' `m5_a_slots_mind_is_read_by_its_own_character_alone` on a `boot_filled` variant used by it alone (the AI fill on): in `Selection` Bo and Cy read `red: 1 (RA): Ada, a mind` and `red fill: a mind` and staff read both; from `SIM START` every reader's board rows pinned whole, none carrying `Doctrine` or a preset word, the seal line to Ada alone, `The fills are not shown once a match starts.` to Bo and Cy, Bo's keyframe `mind` object his own `["Line"]`; after the end `SIM HISTORY` and `SIM REPLAY` name no mind or preset, Ada reads her `Your mind` block over the wire, Bo's report carries none and Cy's is refused; every byte Bo and Cy received, terminal and WebSocket frames alike, is swept for the bare words `Sniper` and `Hunter` and for six- and sixteen-hex-digit words, only Bo's own Selection-time bare `SIM MIND` reply windowed out; discriminating (a leak of the choice to Bo fails it on his terminal and his socket; after the review fixes a post-start board naming the mind to its own pilot, the seal line or the choice told to staff, and closed sockets fail it too), its sweep's rule pinned by `m5_the_fog_sweep_finds_each_leak_it_names`; "no mind named after the start or in the record" read as C-4 rules it (below), with the M5.1 cheap twin's post-start word check. **The last sweep**: every "17b cut M5" comment made true or re-pointed to "the residues cut after 17b's M5" (STOPs A, F, G, K; STOP D's designation half recorded as accepted under D-UM-15), and the stale comments that still handed a released seat "to doctrine" or "to the reflex controller" made true (the production-true "doctrine" phrasings left are listed in 17b's Deviations); the F16 grep (`-P`) reads three lines, this cut's own true sentences, and the twelve comments that read "17b's M5" (the wording of M5.7a's D7), outside its pattern, were each read true (listed in 17b's Evidence). The gate's sweep (the design note's §4.4: fmt, clippy, strict rustdoc, `cargo test --workspace --exclude mush-bot --no-fail-fast` whole as one command, `cargo test -p mush-bot`, the web lane, the three CI-named `--ignored` minds steps, the release harness lanes with the delta without bless, the fan-out lane) green on its first run on the M5.7a tree before its review fixes; the gates those fixes touch (fmt, clippy, the two `mushd` binaries, the transcript, tacsim, the contract tests, strict rustdoc for tacsim, `mushd` and the contract tests, the F16 grep) re-run green on the committed tree; the figures in 17b §3 row M5's Evidence. Nothing moved: no hash, `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT` (10), `FRAME_VERSION` (3), seal digest, `AF1_SHAPE_*` (`811a1ce7…`, 556) or baseline; the five production pins at their `5f505c8` values. Not this gate: the M4 gate's three weekly runs on the blessing host stay the owner's (OQ-12). The cut's record is 17b §3, row M5; its Deviations cell holds the residuals still open (among them the note's D16 and D17, the residues cut — 17b cut M5r — and the rule-language cut, the name of a non-Doctrine seal on the keyframe, the card's slider drawing and the rule glyphs, the production-true "doctrine" phrasings left for M6, D-B10's per-frame cost (16c `T-104`) and the `world_task` flake) and the carries beside them, each to the cut it names.
- **The corrections and rulings 17b cut M5 takes** (doc 17c §2.1 rules each; recorded here, no decision row edited): **C-1** — D-UM-10's "grows to 16 with `GOTO` and `MIND`" is read as 17, with `DRIVE` (17b cut M1a.4) and `MIND` (M5.4). **C-4** — §22's "no mind named after the start or in the record" is §12's "to anyone else": the pilot's own surfaces (their `SIM MIND` read, the seal line, `MIND`, their `Your mind` block), the account that set a side's fill (its `SIM AI MIND` read) and staff (the staff fold, `@mind/set`'s read-back) are not anyone else; "the record" is what other players read — the board from `Boarding` on, `SIM HISTORY`, `SIM REPLAY`, another pilot's `SIM REPORT` — while `MatchRecord.minds` and the tape's `mind_sealed` and `mind_card` lines keep naming the mind by design. **C-5** — §15's and §23's "no mind on a new slot" read as "no mind of the pilot's own": an unchosen slot stays as built, sealed Standard Doctrine on Line (OQ-9 (i)), the board printing nothing for it and `a mind` for a chosen one. **C-6** — §12's "clicking a rule on the web prefills `MIND PROFILE …`" is a profile row's click, which prefills `MIND PROFILE <name>` and sends nothing until Enter (M5.5); what a rule row's click prefills is the cut that seals rules (M6). **C-7** — Doctrine is Standard Doctrine at any preset and flies on release in every match; under Q-UM-15 a non-Doctrine fill flies Standard Doctrine at the lead's chosen preset when it is one of the six, else Line, and a pilot's non-Doctrine choice Standard Doctrine on Line, both taken at the seal (M5.2). **C-8** — "`SIM MIND` prints the arithmetic" (Q-UM-2): a refusal prints §9's sentence in OQ-11's words and the bare read adds the side's half and what is spoken for. **C-10** — the "Carried to M5" index above called itself the one list but omitted the earlier cuts' M5 deferrals (the slot language's four, the eleven `[M2]` terms, odds rows past 2, the over-class refusal, an operator floor above 100, the pool's wording and call-off, `MindSeal.card`'s JSON array, `MindDropped.fault`, "M5 seats one", the lead's fill class); doc 17c §1.3 is the complete list and placed each. **C-11 and C-11a** — the board reads `, flown by its mind` for a drop and an `EXIT` keeps `, left` (§12 said `flown by its mind` "or on `EXIT`"); a sentence about the hull's mind or its seal says sortie (the seal line), one about the seat or the result says match (`EXIT`'s "for the rest of the match", "MIND is off for the match"). **C-12** — §15's first hint ("by your rules") was false in M5, no rule being sealed: it reads `Hint: SIM MIND Standard lets your tank's core fly the Registry's doctrine your way when you climb out.`, and the second hint (on `@mind/save`, naming `@mind/prove`) is M6's (OQ-10 (a)). **C-13** — §12's "with no pin the latest saved revision is resolved at each `SIM START` and printed then" is vacuous before M6: the seal line before `STARTUP` is the one line printed then, and `SIM START` says only Q-UM-15's sentence, only when a substitution happens. **C-14** — §13's "first six hex digits are the seal id players see" is M6's (a revision's `DefinitionDigest`); M5 prints no seal id, and every pilot surface is swept for sixteen-hex-digit words. **C-15** — §9's pool example (`This side would think 96,000 ops a second; its half of the centre's pool is 100,000 and 92,000 are spoken for.`), which adds up under neither reading, reads in OQ-11's words, `This tank would think {would} ops a second; its side's half of the centre's pool is {half} and {spoken} are spoken for.`. **C-16** — §9's "`SIM UNIT`'s check counts no fill and cannot refuse before M5" is stale: it counts the fill since 17b cut M4.3 and every hull that chose no mind at `fill_class`, seated or not, since M5.1, and refuses only under a lowered pool. **C-18** — §12's one line before `STARTUP` is said to a seated pilot whose slot chose a mind (or whose choice was substituted), never for an unchosen slot's default. **C-19** — §11's "picking a preset copies it into the profile; the card then says `Line, edited (min_odds 60)`" is vacuous in M5: no slider editing before M6 (OQ-3). **C-20** — D-UM-44's "a recorded intent at most once per 10 s" is `IntentKind::Profile`, seat-only, on the helm, continuous under its own key and journalled `mind_card` (M5.2). **C-21** — "M5's authored minds" (the M4.3 bullet's "declared for M5's authored minds", the M4.6c "M5, which fields other minds", doc 16's "caps nothing until M5 rates minds" and "an authored mind's draws (M5)") is corrected: nobody authors a mind before M6, M5 fields other minds only through the test seam, and a rating above Class I is the cut that rates a mind above it. **C-22** — §12's card drawing ("one slider a line with a 10-cell bar and the unit-aware value", R-25) and the first three rules' state glyph (R-24) are M6's (the schema-rendered card; the first sealed rule), under OQ-8 (the block shows the seal facts, profile, channels and rules), so M5 draws no card on any surface. **C-23** — §11's R-60 against the seal's floor probe: the probe (the fire section's `min_odds` against the Compact floor) runs only on a hull with a weapons station, the card's pair on every hull, at the choice (M5.1) and the seal (M5.2), so an unarmed hull under a raised floor is never refused — a behaviour change, recorded, on Q-UM-12's default (Standard Doctrine with its fire half absent), relied on and unruled (§21). **C-24, as amended by the S1 ruling** (the orchestrator, 2026-10-11) — over-class is `read_core(&unit.unit) == Some(hw) && hw.governed && rated > hw.class && hw.class > Zero`: **a governed core at Class 0 flies the ROM, as no core does** — one predicate at the choice (`lobby::fit_on_chassis`) and the seal (`seal::seal_for`), so a stuck tank whose core was degraded to Class 0 seals Class 0 as at `5f505c8` and the refusal is fixture-only (Standard Doctrine, rated I, is over no core of Class I or more; built and pinned on `Load III`, OQ-10 (d)). **The question rows' outcomes** (§21): **Q-UM-13** — holdings keyed by the character who boards, confirmed by OQ-2 (M5.1); **Q-UM-15** — ruled by OQ-5: "Doctrine" is Standard Doctrine at any preset, a pilot's sealed Doctrine profile flies on release in every match, and the substitution is built for non-Doctrine seals only and taken at the seal, its four sub-questions answered (the other side's human decided once at `SIM START` from the claimed slots; Doctrine at any preset is Doctrine; the sentence said only when a substitution happens; `SIM RANGE` has no fill and refuses `SIM AI MIND`); **Q-UM-16** — its default confirmed by OQ-4, landing in the rule-language cut right after M5's gate; **Q-UM-3 and D-UM-37** — the lead's fill class deferred to the cut that rates a mind above Class I (OQ-10 (b)), only Class 0 or I meaning anything while every mind is rated I; **Q-UM-2** — the pool visible in the fiction, `SIM MIND` printing the arithmetic as C-8 reads it. **The clock** (the orchestrator's D-B1 ruling at 17b cut M5.4's close): the design note's and its conventions' "`MatchMirror.arena_ms`" for `MIND`'s countdown and pre-check (its M5.4 items 4 and 5) and for the report's span (M5.6) named the wrong field — `arena_ms` is the highest ledger event's stamp, nought for a match's first minutes and lagging a quiet match — and both read `MatchMirror::now_ms`, the arena's own clock as the last `Frames` reported it.
